#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

struct State {
    int node;
    int turn;
};

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    int n, m;
    cin >> n >> m;

    vector<vector<int>> adj(n + 1);
    vector<int> out_degree(n + 1, 0);

    for (int u = 1; u <= n; ++u) {
        int cnt;
        cin >> cnt;
        out_degree[u] = cnt;
        adj[u].resize(cnt);
        for (int j = 0; j < cnt; ++j) {
            cin >> adj[u][j];
        }
    }

    int s;
    cin >> s;

    if (out_degree[s] == 0) {
        cout << "Lose" << endl;
        return 0;
    }

    vector<vector<bool>> visited(n + 1, vector<bool>(2, false));
    vector<vector<pair<int, int>>> parent(n + 1, vector<pair<int, int>>(2, {-1, -1}));

    queue<State> q;
    q.push({s, 0});
    visited[s][0] = true;

    int win_node = -1, win_turn = -1;

    while (!q.empty()) {
        auto [u, t] = q.front();
        q.pop();

        for (int v : adj[u]) {
            int new_t = 1 - t;
            if (!visited[v][new_t]) {
                visited[v][new_t] = true;
                parent[v][new_t] = {u, t};
                q.push({v, new_t});

                if (out_degree[v] == 0 && new_t == 1) {
                    win_node = v;
                    win_turn = new_t;
                    goto found_win;
                }
            }
        }
    }

found_win:
    if (win_node != -1) {
        vector<int> path;
        int current = win_node, turn = win_turn;
        while (current != -1) {
            path.push_back(current);
            auto [prev, prev_turn] = parent[current][turn];
            current = prev;
            turn = prev_turn;
        }
        reverse(path.begin(), path.end());
        cout << "Win\n";
        for (int v : path) cout << v << ' ';
        cout << endl;
        return 0;
    }

    vector<bool> reachable(n + 1, false);
    queue<int> q_reach;
    q_reach.push(s);
    reachable[s] = true;

    while (!q_reach.empty()) {
        int u = q_reach.front();
        q_reach.pop();
        for (int v : adj[u]) {
            if (!reachable[v]) {
                reachable[v] = true;
                q_reach.push(v);
            }
        }
    }

    vector<int> in_degree(n + 1, 0);
    for (int u = 1; u <= n; ++u) {
        if (reachable[u]) {
            for (int v : adj[u]) {
                if (reachable[v]) in_degree[v]++;
            }
        }
    }

    queue<int> kahn;
    int processed = 0;
    for (int u = 1; u <= n; ++u) {
        if (reachable[u] && in_degree[u] == 0) kahn.push(u);
    }

    while (!kahn.empty()) {
        int u = kahn.front();
        kahn.pop();
        processed++;
        for (int v : adj[u]) {
            if (reachable[v] && --in_degree[v] == 0) kahn.push(v);
        }
    }

    int total = count(reachable.begin(), reachable.end(), true);
    if (processed < total) cout << "Draw\n";
    else cout << "Lose\n";

    return 0;

First term: derivative of \(1/(2a + b)^2\) with respect to a is \(-2/(2a + b)^3 * 2 = -4/(2a + b)^3\).

The last term: derivative of \(-1/(ab + bc + ca)\) with respect to a is \( (b + c)/(ab + bc + ca)^2 \).

Similarly, partial derivatives with respect to b and c would be similar with cyclic permutations.

Similarly for variable b:

And for variable c:

Basemap URL domains have fastly.net (e.g. here: https://github.com/CartoDB/cartoframes/blob/master/cartoframes/layer.py#L63), this should be replaced by basemaps.cartocdn.com in whole code and tests:
* Old: https://cartodb-basemaps-{s}.global.ssl.fastly.net/
* New: https://{s}.basemaps.cartocdn.com/

Other URL parts remain same. Needed for https://github.com/CartoDB/lbs-services/issues/16

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

struct Point {
    int x, y;
    Point() {}
    Point(int x, int y) : x(x), y(y) {}
    bool operator==(const Point& p) const { return x == p.x && y == p.y; }
};

vector<Point> points;
vector<Point> hull;
int N, K;
vector<vector<Point>> validHulls;

// Cross product to determine the orientation of the triplet (o, a, b)
int cross(const Point& o, const Point& a, const Point& b) {
    return (a.x - o.x) * (b.y - o.y) - (a.y - o.y) * (b.x - o.x);
}

// Polar angle comparison
bool compare(const Point& a, const Point& b) {
    int c = cross(points[0], a, b);
    if (c == 0) {
        return (a.x - points[0].x)*(a.x - points[0].x) + (a.y - points[0].y)*(a.y - points[0].y) < 
               (b.x - points[0].x)*(b.x - points[0].x) + (b.y - points[0].y)*(b.y - points[0].y);
    }
    return c > 0;
}

// Check if point p is on the segment (a, b)
bool onSegment(Point a, Point p, Point b) {
    return min(a.x, b.x) <= p.x && p.x <= max(a.x, b.x) &&
           min(a.y, b.y) <= p.y && p.y <= max(a.y, b.y) &&
           cross(a, p, b) == 0;
}

// Check if point p is inside the convex polygon (using ray casting)
bool isInside(const vector<Point>& poly, Point p) {
    int n = poly.size();
    if (n == 0) return false;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        if (p == poly[i] || onSegment(poly[i], p, poly[(i+1)%n])) {
            return false;
        }
    }
    bool inside = false;
    for (int i = 0, j = n-1; i < n; j = i++) {
        if (((poly[i].y > p.y) != (poly[j].y > p.y)) &&
            (p.x < (poly[j].x - poly[i].x) * (p.y - poly[i].y) / (double)(poly[j].y - poly[i].y) + poly[i].x))
            inside = !inside;
    }
    return inside;
}

// Generate all convex hulls of size K and check if they are empty
void generateCombinations(int start, vector<Point>& current) {
    if (current.size() == K) {
        vector<Point> temp = current;
        vector<Point> convHull;
        int m = temp.size();
        if (m <= 2) return;
        sort(temp.begin(), temp.end(), [](const Point& a, const Point& b) {
            return a.x < b.x || (a.x == b.x && a.y < b.y);
        });
        vector<Point> up, down;
        for (auto& p : temp) {
            while (up.size() >= 2 && cross(up[up.size()-2], up.back(), p) >= 0)
                up.pop_back();
            up.push_back(p);
            while (down.size() >= 2 && cross(down[down.size()-2], down.back(), p) <= 0)
                down.pop_back();
            down.push_back(p);
        }
        convHull = up;
        for (int i = down.size()-2; i > 0; --i)
            convHull.push_back(down[i]);
        if (convHull.size() != K) return;
        bool valid = true;
        for (auto& p : points) {
            if (find(temp.begin(), temp.end(), p) != temp.end()) continue;
            if (isInside(convHull, p)) {
                valid = false;
                break;
            }
        }
        if (valid) {
            validHulls.push_back(convHull);
        }
        return;
    }
    for (int i = start; i < points.size(); ++i) {
        current.push_back(points[i]);
        generateCombinations(i+1, current);
        current.pop_back();
    }
}

double computeArea(const vector<Point>& poly) {
    double area = 0.0;
    int n = poly.size();
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        int j = (i + 1) % n;
        area += (poly[i].x * poly[j].y - poly[j].x * poly[i].y);
    }
    return fabs(area) / 2.0;
}

int main() {
    cin >> N >> K;
    points.resize(N);
    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        cin >> points[i].x >> points[i].y;
    }
    if (K < 3) {
        cout << "0.00" << endl;
        return 0;
    }
    vector<Point> current;
    generateCombinations(0, current);
    double maxArea = 0.0;
    for (auto& hull : validHulls) {
        double area = computeArea(hull);
        if (area > maxArea) {
            maxArea = area;
        }
    }
    cout << fixed << setprecision(2) << maxArea << endl;
    return 0;

The total <#sep#>add base url validation on `https` to ensure all requests occur as expected
`POST api/v1/imports` forwards to `GET api/v1/imports` if the base url is not `https`. This causes some unexpected behaviors and perplexing errors, so we need to add base url validation to ensure all the services work as expected. We can bundle this into the `is_authenticated` method in CartoContext.

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

struct Point {
    int x, y;
};

long long compute_min_split(const vector<Point>& points) {
    int n = points.size();
    if (n <= 1) return 0;

    vector<int> prefix_min_x(n), prefix_max_x(n), prefix_min_y(n), prefix_max_y(n);
    prefix_min_x[0] = points[0].x;
    prefix_max_x[0] = points[0].x;
    prefix_min_y[0] = points[0].y;
    prefix_max_y[0] = points[0].y;

    for (int i = 1; i < n; ++i) {
        prefix_min_x[i] = min(prefix_min_x[i-1], points[i].x);
        prefix_max_x[i] = max(prefix_max_x[i-1], points[i].x);
        prefix_min_y[i] = min(prefix_min_y[i-1], points[i].y);
        prefix_max_y[i] = max(prefix_max_y[i-1], points[i].y);
    }

    vector<int> suffix_min_x(n), suffix_max_x(n), suffix_min_y(n), suffix_max_y(n);
    suffix_min_x[n-1] = points[n-1].x;
    suffix_max_x[n-1] = points[n-1].x;
    suffix_min_y[n-1] = points[n-1].y;
    suffix_max_y[n-1] = points[n-1].y;

    for (int i = n-2; i >= 0; --i) {
        suffix_min_x[i] = min(suffix_min_x[i+1], points[i].x);
        suffix_max_x[i] = max(suffix_max_x[i+1], points[i].x);
        suffix_min_y[i] = min(suffix_min_y[i+1], points[i].y);
        suffix_max_y[i] = max(suffix_max_y[i+1], points[i].y);
    }

    long long res = LLONG_MAX;
    for (int i = 1; i < n; ++i) {
        long long left_area = (prefix_max_x[i-1] - prefix_min_x[i-1]) * 1LL * (prefix_max_y[i-1] - prefix_min_y[i-1]);
        long long right_area = (suffix_max_x[i] - suffix_min_x[i]) * (suffix_max_y[i] - suffix_min_y[i]);
        res = min(res, left_area + right_area);
    }
    return res;
}

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    int t;
    cin >> t;
    while (t--) {
        int n;
        cin >> n;
        vector<Point> points(n);
        for (auto& p : points) {
            cin >> p.x >> p.y;
        }

        if (n == 1) {
            cout << "0\n";
            continue;
        }

        int min_x = points[0].x, max_x = points[0].x;
        int min_y = points[0].y, max_y = points[0].y;
        for (const auto& p : points) {
            min_x = min(min_x, p.x);
            max_x = max(max_x, p.x);
            min_y = min(min_y, p.y);
            max_y = max(max_y, p.y);
        }
        long long single_area = (max_x - min_x) * 1LL * (max_y - min_y);

        vector<Point> sorted_x = points;
        sort(sorted_x.begin(), sorted_x.end(), [](const Point& a, const Point& b) {
            return a.x < b.x || (a.x == b.x && a.y < b.y);
        });
        long long x_split = compute_min_split(sorted_x);

        vector<Point> sorted_y = points;
        sort(sorted_y.begin(), sorted_y.end(), [](const Point& a, const Point& b) {
            return a.y < b.y || (a.y == b.y && a.x < b.x);
        });
        long long y_split = compute_min_split(sorted_y);

        cout << min(single_area, min(x_split, y_split)) << '\n';
    }
    return 0;
}<#sep#>.

Alternatively, maybe think of the variables in terms of ratios. Let me set a = k*b and c = m*b, so that all variables are expressed in terms of b. Let me try that.

Let me set a = k*b, c = m*b. Then, since all variables are positive, k and m are positive real numbers.

So, substituting into the expression:

First term: 1/(2a + b)^2 =1/(2k b + b)^2 =1/[b²(2k +1)^2] =1/[b²(2k +1)^2].

Second term:1/(2b + c)^2 =1/(2b + m b)^2 =1/[b²(2 +m)^2].

Third term:1/(2c +a)^2 =1/(2m b +k b)^2 =1/[b²(2m +k)^2].

The denominator term:ab + bc + ca = (k b *b) + (b * m b) + (m b *k b) = b²(k + m + km).

Therefore, the entire expression can be written as:

[1/( (2k +1)^2 ) + 1/( (2 + m)^2 ) + 1/( (2m +k)^2 ) ] / b² - 1/[b²(k + m + km)].

Since b is a positive real number, to minimize the expression, we can set b to any positive value, but since all terms have 1/b², the expression is independent of b. Therefore, the value is the same for any b, so we can set b =1 for simplicity. Therefore, the expression reduces to:

[1/(2k +1)^2 +1/(2 + m)^2 +1/(2m +k)^2] - 1/(k + m + km).

So now, the problem reduces to minimizing this expression over positive real numbers k and m. Hmm, this might be easier. Let me denote variables as k and m, so the problem is now two-dimensional.

First terms:

1/(2t +1)^2 +1/(2 + t)^2 +1/(2t + t)^2 = 1/(2t +1)^2 +1/(2 + t)^2 +1/(3t)^2.

Denominator term: t + t + t^2 = 2t + t².

So the expression is:

[1/(2t +1)^2 +1/(2 + t)^2 +1/(3t)^2] - 1/(2t + t²).

Total expression≈0.1303 -0.125≈0.0053, still positive.

Sum≈0.25+0.16+0.444≈0.854.

Total expression≈0.854 -0.8≈0.054, still positive.

If a dataset is written to carto with `CartoContext.write`, the schema will be updated using `alter table alter column` statements. But if the only columns are carto utility columns the_geom and cartodb_id, this schema-update shouldn't happen.

+# schema definition functions
+def dtypes2pg(dtype):
+    """Returns equivalent PostgreSQL type for input `dtype`"""
+    mapping = {
+        'float64': 'numeric',
+        'int64': 'numeric',
+        'float32': 'numeric',
+        'int32': 'numeric',
+        'object': 'text',
+        'bool': 'boolean',
+        'datetime64[ns]': 'timestamp',
+    }
+    return mapping.get(str(dtype), 'text')
+
+
+# NOTE: this is not currently used anywhere
+def pg2dtypes(pgtype):
+    """Returns equivalent dtype for input `pgtype`."""
+    mapping = {
+        'date': 'datetime64[ns]',
+        'number': 'float64',
+        'string': 'object',
+        'boolean': 'bool',
+        'geometry': 'object',
+    }
+    return mapping.get(str(pgtype), 'object')
+
+
+def df2pg_schema(dataframe, pgcolnames):
+    """Print column names with PostgreSQL schema for the SELECT statement of
+    a SQL query"""
+    util_cols = set(('the_geom', 'the_geom_webmercator', 'cartodb_id'))
+    if set(dataframe.columns).issubset(util_cols):
+        return ', '.join(dataframe.columns)
+    schema = ', '.join([
+        'NULLIF("{col}", \'\')::{t} AS {col}'.format(col=c,
+                                                     t=dtypes2pg(t))
+        for c, t in zip(pgcolnames, dataframe.dtypes)
+        if c not in util_cols])
+    if 'the_geom' in pgcolnames:
+        return '"the_geom", ' + schema
+    return schema
